<script>

  
 	 var customer_logged_in = false;
  

    $(document).on('click', '.clear-cart', function(){
      $.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: '/cart/clear',
        success: function() {
          window.location.reload();
        }
      });
  });

  var clicked = false;
  $(document).on('click', '#AddToCart, .Add-To-Cart, .add-to-cart, .add_to_cart, .AddToCart, .ADDTOCART, .add, .addToCart, [name=add]', function(e) {
    if(!clicked && customer_logged_in) {
      e.preventDefault();
      clicked = true;
      checkForTokenAttr(false, function(){
      	$(this).trigger('click');
      }.bind(this));
	}
  })

  checkForTokenAttr = function(overwrite, callback) {
    $.ajax({
      url: '/cart.js',
      type: 'get',
      dataType: 'json',
      success: function(result) {
        var cart_obj = result;
        checkForTokenAttrCallback(cart_obj, overwrite, callback);
      }
    });
  }

  function checkForTokenAttrCallback(cart_obj, overwrite, callback) {
    if(customer_logged_in) {
      var token_saved = false;
      if (!overwrite){
        for(var attr in cart_obj.attributes) {
          if (attr == 'token_saved' ) {
            token_saved = true;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!token_saved) {
        $.ajax({
          url:'/cart/update',
          type: 'post',
          dataType: 'json',
          data:{
            attributes:{
              token_saved: true
            }
          }
        });

        $.ajax({
          url:'https://cart-keeper.boldapps.net/front_end/customer_api/create_update_persistent_cart',
          type: 'post',
          dataType: 'json',
          data:{
            customer_id: customer_id,
            shop: Shopify.shop,
            cart_token: cart_obj['token'],
            cart_total: cart_obj['total_price']/100,
            user_agent: navigator.userAgent
          },
          success: function(message) {
            if(callback){
            	callback();
            }
          },
          error: function(message) {
            console.warn('Error from the Cart Keeper app', message);
          }
        });
      }
      else {
      	callback();
      }
    }
    else {
    	callback();
    }
  }
</script>

/*
===================================
| DATALAYER ARCHITECTURE: SHOPIFY |
-----------------------------------
DEFINITION:
A data layer helps you collect more accurate analytics data, that in turn allows you to better understand what potential buyers are doing on your website and where you can make improvements. It also reduces the time to implement marketing tags on a website, and reduces the need for IT involvement, leaving them to get on with implementing new features and fixing bugs.
RESOURCES:
http://www.datalayerdoctor.com/a-gentle-introduction-to-the-data-layer-for-digital-marketers/
http://www.simoahava.com/analytics/data-layer/
AUTHORS:
Mechelle Warneke = [{
Email: mechellewarneke@gmail.com,

  Email: mechelle@bvaccel.com,
  Position: XO Strategist | Technical Web Analyst
}]
}];
Tyler Shambora = [{

  Position: Lead Web Developer
}]
}];
EXTERNAL DEPENDENCIES:
* jQuery
* jQuery Cookie Plugin v1.4.1 - https://github.com/carhartl/jquery-cookie

DataLayer Architecture: Shopify v1.3.1

*/

// search parameters
getURLParams = function(name, url){
    if (!url) url = window.location.href;
    name = name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, "\\$&");
    var regex = new RegExp("[?&]" + name + "(=([^&#]*)|&|#|$)"),
    results = regex.exec(url);
    if (!results) return null;
    if (!results[2]) return '';
    return decodeURIComponent(results[2].replace(/\+/g, " "));
  };

| DYNAMIC DEPENDENCIES |

  wishlist: null,
  removeCart: null
};

customBindings = {
  cartTriggers: [],

  removeCartTrigger: [],

  wishlistPage: []
};

// stitch bindings
objectArray = customBindings;
outputObject = __bva__;

applyBindings = function(objectArray, outputObject){
  for (var x in objectArray) {  
    var key = x;
    var objs = objectArray[x]; 
    values = [];    
    if(objs.length > 0){    
      values.push(objs);
      if(key in outputObject){              
        values.push(outputObject[key]); 
        outputObject[key] = values.join(", "); 
      }else{        
        outputObject[key] = values.join(", ");
      }   
    }  
  }
};

applyBindings(customBindings, __bva__);
applyBindings(defaultBindings, __bva__);

<script>
  // We are setting the top on the flyout items in the sidebar with the goal of the items never falling off the screen.
  $(function () {
    $('.site-nav--with-dropdown').on('mouseenter', function () {
        var el = $(this).find('.site-nav--dropdown-list')
        if ($(this).find('.site-nav--dropdown-list li').length) {
          var scrollTop = $(window).scrollTop();
          var elementOffset = el.offset().top;
          var elChildHeight = el.find('li').first().height();
          var distance = Math.ceil((elementOffset - scrollTop) / 1.5);
          if(distance > el.height()){
            el.attr("style", "top:-" + ((el.height() - elChildHeight)) + "px;");
          }
          else{
            el.attr("style", "top:-" + (distance-30) + "px;");
          }
        }
      })
      .on('mouseleave', function () {
        $(this).find('.site-nav--dropdown-list').attr("style", "top:0px;");
      });
  });


</script>

<script>
  var ISPRecommendedProvider = (function () {

    /**
    * @param {Number} storeId The ID of the store that identifies this store in ISP's database
    * @param {string} uuid
    */
    function T (storeId, uuid) {
      this.storeId = storeId
      this.uuid = uuid
    };

    /**
    * Gets recommended products based on the product and variant id passed in. Callback will be called once the recommended
    * products have been fetched.
    * 
    * @param {Number} productId
    * @param {Number} variantId 
    * @param {function} callback
    * @param {Object} options
    */
    T.prototype.getProductRecommendations = function (productId, variantId, callback, options) {
      options = options || {}
      maxProducts = options.maxProducts || 3

      var specs = [{ "sources": "similar_products_by_attributes,similar_products,similar_products_lookalike", "max_suggest": maxProducts.toString() }];
      var data = {
        specs: JSON.stringify(specs),
        products: JSON.stringify([productId]),
        product_id: productId,
        store_id: this.storeId,
        UUID: this.uuid
      }
  
      // Getting recommendations from brain
      $.ajax({
        url: '//ultimate-dot-acp-magento.appspot.com/related_products_suggest',
        data: data,
        dataType: 'jsonp',
        success: function (data) {
  
          // Getting just the handles of the offers
          var handles = data.widget_responses[0].products.map(function (product) {
            var url = product.u;
            return url.match(/products\/(.+)$/)[1]; 
          });
  
          // Fetching product data
          BOLD.BMS.TCF.Common.fetchMultipleProductsByHandles(handles.slice(0, maxProducts), callback);
        }
      });
    };

    return T;
  })();

  (function () {
    var instance = new ISPRecommendedProvider(18326341, '799d377f-46e5-4dfc-b5cb-967232a5317e')

    BOLD.BMS.TCF.Recommended.registerRecommendedProvider('isp', instance)
    BOLD.BMS.TCF.AjaxCart.registerRecommendedProvider('isp', instance)
  })();
</script>
<script>

  var CBBRecommendedProvider = (function ($) {

    /**
    * @param {string} shop The permanant domain for the store
    */
    function T (shop) {
      this.shop = shop;
    };

    /**
    * Gets recommended products based on the product and variant id passed in. Callback will be called once the recommended
    * products have been fetched.
    *
    * @param {Number} productId
    * @param {Number} variantId
    * @param {function} callback
    * @param {Object} options
    */
    T.prototype.getProductRecommendations = function (productId, variantId, callback, options) {
      options = options || {};
      maxProducts = options.maxProducts || 3;

      var data = {
        errorPage: false,
        shop: this.shop,
        cart: false,
        'productIdList[]': productId,
        activeCurrencyCode: 'USD',
        path: ''
      };

      // Getting recommendations from brain
      $.ajax({
        url: '//www.codeblackbelt.com/json/preferences/also-bought.json',
        data: data,
        dataType: 'json',
        success: function (data) {

          // Getting just the handles of the offers
          var handles = data.preferences[0].products.map(function (product) {
            return product.url;
          });

          // Fetching product data
          BOLD.BMS.TCF.Common.fetchMultipleProductsByHandles(handles.slice(0, maxProducts), callback);
        }
      });
    };

    return T;
  })(BOLD.jQuery || jQuery);

  (function () {
    var instance = new CBBRecommendedProvider('tableclothsfactory-com.myshopify.com');

    BOLD.BMS.TCF.Recommended.registerRecommendedProvider('cbb', instance);
    BOLD.BMS.TCF.AjaxCart.registerRecommendedProvider('cbb', instance);
  })();

<script>
    var StampedSiteBadgeFn = (function (window, document) {
        function init(data, obj, $) {
            var height = $(obj).attr('data-height') || 120;
            var title = $(obj).attr('data-title') || 'VERIFIED REVIEWS';
            var titleSize = $(obj).attr('data-title-size') || '12';

            var colorOuter = $(obj).attr('data-color-outer');
            var colorInner = $(obj).attr('data-color-inner');
            var colorRibbon = $(obj).attr('data-color-ribbon');

            var styleColorText = $(obj).attr('data-color-text');
            var styleColorStar = $(obj).attr('data-color-stars');
            var styleSizeStar = $(obj).attr('data-size-stars');

            var totalType = $(obj).attr('data-total-type');

            var badgeType = $(obj).attr('data-badge-type') || 'badge';

            var isTotalWidget = obj.data('is-widget-total') == true ? true : false;

            var isHidePowered = obj.data('is-hide-powered') == true ? true : false;

            // if data empty, hide widget
            if (data.length <= 0) {
                //$(obj).hide();
                //return;
            } else {
                //$(obj).show();
            }

            var totalAll = data.totalAll;

            if (data.totalAllWithNPS > 0 && !totalType) {
                totalAll = data.totalAllWithNPS;
            }

            if (isTotalWidget == true) {
                totalAll = data.total;
            }

            if (!badgeType || badgeType == 'badge') {
                $('#stamped-badge-2', obj).remove();
            }

	    if (isHidePowered){
                $('.stamped-badge-powered', obj).remove();
	    }

            else if (badgeType == 'minimal')
            {
                $('#stamped-badge-3', obj).remove();

                var starHtml = "";

                var totalRating = data.ratingAll;

                while (totalRating >= 1) {
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star"></i>';
                    totalRating--;
                }

                while (totalRating > 0) {
                    if (totalRating >= 0.5 && totalRating < 1) {
                        starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-half-o"></i>';
                    } else {
                        starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-o"></i>';
                    }
                    totalRating--;
                }

                var eleRating = $('<div class="stamped-reviews-rating stamped-style-color-star"> ' + starHtml + '</div>');
                $('#stamped-badge-2 .stamped-badge-stars', obj).append(eleRating);

                var classNameWidget = '#stamped-reviews-widget[data-widget-type="site-badge"]';
                var style = '';

                if (styleColorText) {
                    style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-text { color:' + styleColorText + ' !important; }';
                }
                if (styleColorStar) {
                    style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-star i { color:' + styleColorStar + ' !important; }';
                }
                if (styleSizeStar) {
                    style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-size-star i:before { font-size:' + styleSizeStar + 'px !important; }';
                }

                if (style) {
                    $(obj).append('<style>' + style + '</style>');
                }
            }

            if (totalAll) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]', obj).html(+totalAll);

                if (data.totalAll < 10) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 80);
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 10 && data.totalAll <= 99) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 67);
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 1000 && data.totalAll < 10000) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 45);
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 10000) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-count-color"]').attr('font-size', 33);
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 42);
                }

                if (data.totalAll < 200) {
                    $('#badge-1').show();
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 200 && data.totalAll <= 499) {
                    $('#badge-2').show();
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 500) {
                    $('#badge-3').show();
                }

                $('#stamped-badge-2, #stamped-badge-3', obj).attr('data-rating', data.ratingAll);
            }

            if (height) {
                $('#stamped-badge-3', obj).height(height);
            }

            if (title) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-title"]', obj).text(title).css('font-size', titleSize + 'px');
            }

            if (colorRibbon) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-ribbon-foreground"], [id="stamped-badge-ribbon-background"]', obj).attr('fill', colorRibbon);
            }

            if (colorOuter) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-outer"], [id="stamped-badge-border"]', obj).attr('fill', colorOuter);
            }

            if (colorInner) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-inner"], #stamped-badge-inner use[fill]', obj).attr('fill', colorInner);
            }

            if (styleColorText) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-count-color"]', obj).attr('fill', styleColorText);
            }

            if (styleColorStar) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-stars-color"]', obj).attr('fill', styleColorStar);
            }
        }

        return {
            init: init
        }
    }(window, document));</script>

<script>
    var StampedSiteBadgeFn = (function (window, document) {
        function init(data, obj, $) {
            var height = $(obj).attr('data-height') || 120;
            var title = $(obj).attr('data-title') || 'VERIFIED REVIEWS';
            var titleSize = $(obj).attr('data-title-size') || '12';

            var colorOuter = $(obj).attr('data-color-outer');
            var colorInner = $(obj).attr('data-color-inner');
            var colorRibbon = $(obj).attr('data-color-ribbon');

            var styleColorText = $(obj).attr('data-color-text');
            var styleColorStar = $(obj).attr('data-color-stars');
            var styleSizeStar = $(obj).attr('data-size-stars');

            var totalType = $(obj).attr('data-total-type');

            var badgeType = $(obj).attr('data-badge-type') || 'badge';

            var isTotalWidget = obj.data('is-widget-total') == true ? true : false;

            var isHidePowered = obj.data('is-hide-powered') == true ? true : false;

            // if data empty, hide widget
            if (data.length <= 0) {
                //$(obj).hide();
                //return;
            } else {
                //$(obj).show();
            }

            var totalAll = data.totalAll;

            if (data.totalAllWithNPS > 0 && !totalType) {
                totalAll = data.totalAllWithNPS;
            }

            if (isTotalWidget == true) {
                totalAll = data.total;
            }

            if (!badgeType || badgeType == 'badge') {
                $('#stamped-badge-2', obj).remove();
            }

	    if (isHidePowered){
                $('.stamped-badge-powered', obj).remove();
	    }

            else if (badgeType == 'minimal')
            {
                $('#stamped-badge-3', obj).remove();

                var starHtml = "";

                var totalRating = data.ratingAll;

                while (totalRating >= 1) {
                    starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star"></i>';
                    totalRating--;
                }

                while (totalRating > 0) {
                    if (totalRating >= 0.5 && totalRating < 1) {
                        starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-half-o"></i>';
                    } else {
                        starHtml += '<i class="stamped-fa stamped-fa-star-o"></i>';
                    }
                    totalRating--;
                }

                var eleRating = $('<div class="stamped-reviews-rating stamped-style-color-star"> ' + starHtml + '</div>');
                $('#stamped-badge-2 .stamped-badge-stars', obj).append(eleRating);

                var classNameWidget = '#stamped-reviews-widget[data-widget-type="site-badge"]';
                var style = '';

                if (styleColorText) {
                    style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-text { color:' + styleColorText + ' !important; }';
                }
                if (styleColorStar) {
                    style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-color-star i { color:' + styleColorStar + ' !important; }';
                }
                if (styleSizeStar) {
                    style += classNameWidget + ' ' + '.stamped-style-size-star i:before { font-size:' + styleSizeStar + 'px !important; }';
                }

                if (style) {
                    $(obj).append('<style>' + style + '</style>');
                }
            }

            if (totalAll) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]', obj).html(+totalAll);

                if (data.totalAll < 10) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 80);
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 10 && data.totalAll <= 99) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 67);
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 1000 && data.totalAll < 10000) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 45);
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 10000) {
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-count-color"]').attr('font-size', 33);
                    $('[id="stamped-badge-total"]').attr('x', 42);
                }

                if (data.totalAll < 200) {
                    $('#badge-1').show();
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 200 && data.totalAll <= 499) {
                    $('#badge-2').show();
                } else if (data.totalAll >= 500) {
                    $('#badge-3').show();
                }

                $('#stamped-badge-2, #stamped-badge-3', obj).attr('data-rating', data.ratingAll);
            }

            if (height) {
                $('#stamped-badge-3', obj).height(height);
            }

            if (title) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-title"]', obj).text(title).css('font-size', titleSize + 'px');
            }

            if (colorRibbon) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-ribbon-foreground"], [id="stamped-badge-ribbon-background"]', obj).attr('fill', colorRibbon);
            }

            if (colorOuter) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-outer"], [id="stamped-badge-border"]', obj).attr('fill', colorOuter);
            }

            if (colorInner) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-inner"], #stamped-badge-inner use[fill]', obj).attr('fill', colorInner);
            }

            if (styleColorText) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-count-color"]', obj).attr('fill', styleColorText);
            }

            if (styleColorStar) {
                $('[id="stamped-badge-stars-color"]', obj).attr('fill', styleColorStar);
            }
        }

        return {
            init: init
        }
    }(window, document));</script>
</div></a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</main>

  function highliteCollection(){
    var parentCollection = $('#uncomplicated-breadcrumbs span').eq(0).next().attr('title');
    $('.sidebar-list li').each(function(){
      var collectionTitle = $(this).find('a').first();
      if(parentCollection && collectionTitle.text().trim() === parentCollection.trim()){
        collectionTitle.css({'background': '#879eb2', 'color': '#fff'});
        return false;
      }
    });
  }

  function checkForCrumbs(){
    if(!$('#uncomplicated-breadcrumbs').length){
      window.setTimeout(function(){
        checkForCrumbs()
        return;
      }, 250)
    }
    highliteCollection();
  }

  checkForCrumbs();


  </script>

<script>
      function collectionImageHover(){
        $('.product-grid-item').on('mouseenter', function () {
            var img = $(this).find('[data-hover-src]').attr('data-hover-src');
            if (img == "blank") {
              return;
            }
          $(this).find('.featured-img.with-featured-hover').attr('src', img);
        })
        .on('mouseleave', function () {
          var img = $(this).find('.product-grid-image--centered [data-original]').attr('data-original');
          $(this).find('.featured-img.with-featured-hover').attr('src', img);
        });
      }
      
        collectionImageHover();
      
</script>

     } else {

     }

  });

<script>

    function waitForDependency(dep, i, callback) {
      i = i || 0;

      if (i > 50) return;
      setTimeout(function () {

        try { if (typeof eval(dep) === 'undefined') throw new Error(); } catch(error) { waitForDependency(dep, ++i, callback); return; }

        callback();
      }, i == 0 ? 0 : 250);
    }

    // load Instant Search Plus
    $(window).load(function() {
      waitForDependency('_isp_js_inject', 0, function () {
        _isp_js_inject(_isp_js_load_https + "/js/isp.jquery.requires.full.js", js_callback);
      });
    });
  </script>

<script>
  window.addEventListener("klaviyoForms", function(e) { 
    if (e.detail.type == 'submit') {
      ga('send', 'event', 'Klaviyo form', 'form_submit', e.detail.formId);

<script>
  var isBulkCustomer = sessionStorage.getItem('isBulkCustomer');

  if(isBulkCustomer == null){
    sessionStorage.setItem('isBulkCustomer', false);
    ga('set', 'dimension1', 0);
    ga('send', {
      hitType: 'event',
      eventCategory: 'BulkCustomer',
      eventAction: 'PageVisit',
      nonInteraction: true
    })
  }
  
  // Tracking when a customer clicks the quick view button
  $(document).on('click', '.quick_shop', function () {
    ga('send', {
      hitType: 'event',
      eventCategory: 'Quickview',
      eventAction: 'QuickviewViewed'
    });
  });

  // Tracking when a customer clicks the bulk discount modal link
  $(document).on('click', '.eastsideco_sizeGuides a', function () {
    ga('send', {
      hitType: 'event',
      eventCategory: 'BulkCustomer',
      eventAction: 'BulkDiscountViewed',
      eventLabel: ''
    });
  });

  // Tracking cart adds, updates, and changes
  $(document).ajaxSuccess(function (event, readyState, args) {
    if (!args || !args.url.match(/^\/cart\/(?:add|update|change).js(?:on)?$/)) return

    // Getting cart because the item data returned from the add/update/change
    // event does not yet have any modifications from Shopify scripts
    $.ajax({
      url: '/cart.js',
      dataType: 'json',
      success: function (cart) {

        // Looking through cart items to see if there are any bulk discounts
        // applied to any of the items
        var discountInCart = cart.items.some(function (item) {
          return item.discounts.length > 0;
        });

        sessionStorage.setItem('isBulkCustomer', discountInCart ? true : false);
        ga('set', 'dimension1', discountInCart ? 1 : 0);

        ga('send', {
          hitType: 'event',
          eventCategory: 'BulkCustomer',
          eventAction: 'CartChange',
          eventLabel: discountInCart ? 'yes' : 'no'
        });
      }
    });
  });
</script>
